# Source register

Appendix R lists every file of the consolidation set; Appendix S gives the dialect of each export and journal;
Appendix H is part A of the host register. Control records: csv and tsv first line #ctl; jsonl first line object _ctl;
json key _ctl; xml root attributes ctl-id, ctl-prev, ctl-rows; xlsx and docx core property subject; parquet schema
metadata ctl_id, ctl_prev, ctl_rows; sqlite view _control; yaml key _ctl; toml table _ctl; ini section [_ctl]; md and
html comment <!-- ctl ... -->; pdf first line CONTROL; log first line # ctl; fixed-width first line CTL; mbox headers
X-Ctl-Id, X-Ctl-Prev and X-Ctl-Rows of the first message; ics properties X-CTL-ID, X-CTL-PREV, X-CTL-ROWS; zip member
CONTROL.

## Format conventions

- Header drift: a csv, tsv or xlsx table may repeat its header with a different column order (and renamed columns,
  e.g. note becomes remark, status becomes state); following rows use the new header. jsonl uses a line
  {"_schema": {"rename": {...}}}; json uses a parallel list named <list>_v2 with <list>_v2_renames; xml uses a
  schema-change element with a renames attribute; yaml and toml use a list <name>_v2; ini uses a [__layout__] section;
  sqlite uses a second table <name>_v2; md, html and docx start a second table; pdf repeats the COLUMNS line with the
  new order; fixed-width files use a new #LAYOUT line; logs use # FORMAT v2 renames ...; mbox uses an X-Schema header;
  ics uses X-SCHEMA:2 (X-HOLD-PREFIX becomes X-PREFIX).
- Fixed-width files: #LAYOUT gives 1-based inclusive character columns. #ENC switches the byte encoding of the following
  lines (latin-1 or utf-8). A path field ending with + continues on following lines that start with +.
- tsv files escape \t, \n, \r and \\ inside fields. inv_fin02_q3.tsv gives each line's byte encoding in its enc column.
- pdf tables: cells are separated by ' | '; \| is a literal |, \\ a literal backslash, \uXXXX a character outside
  Latin-1; a cell holding only ~ is empty. Every page repeats its COLUMNS line.
- ini files start with a UTF-8 byte order mark, may contain % and ; in values, and keep option names case-sensitive.
- ics files fold long lines (a line starting with a space continues the previous one) and escape \, \; and \\.
- mbox bodies may be quoted-printable or base64; body lines starting with >From are unescaped by dropping the >.
- log fields are key=value; quoted values use \" and \\ escapes.
- OBJ01 listing: rows with is_latest=false or delete_marker=true are not files (ops-runbook.md).

## Sentinels

Unknown size: -1 (inv_eng01_q3.csv, inv_obj01_q3_r2.csv), ? (inv_eng02_q3.txt), 9999 (inv_hr01_q3.xlsx),
******* (arc01_tape_catalog.txt), - (rnd01_cold_listing.pdf, inv_ops01_q3.tsv), empty or null elsewhere.
Owner not captured: S-1-0-0, uid:<host>:65534, nobody@corp.example, CORP\-, OLDCORP\-, -, svc-none, MF?????,
64 zeros (S3), and N/A. Digest not computed: all zeros.

## Appendix S. Export dialects

| file | volume | time | size | digest | owner | notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| arc01_ltfs_index.db | ARC01 | iso_z | int | sha1 | mf | table ltfs_files |
| arc01_tape_catalog.txt | ARC01 | julian | unit | sha1 | mf | fixed width; flag X = spanned continuation; file_id from arc01_ltfs_index.db |
| inv_eng01_q3.csv | ENG01 | filetime | grouped | sha256 | sid | UTF-8 with BOM; size may be grouped with commas; file_id hex |
| inv_eng01_q3_deep.jsonl | ENG01 | iso_z | int | sha256 | sid | first lines _ctl and _export |
| inv_eng01_q3_part2.db | ENG01 | epoch_ms | int | sha256 | sid\|corp | table inventory (and inventory_v2); owner SID or CORP login; NULL bytes = unknown |
| inv_eng02_q3.txt | ENG02 | local | int | md5 | corp\|oldcorp | fixed width; local time of the volume zone |
| inv_fin01_q3.parquet | FIN01 | epoch_ns | unit | sha256 | uid | size string in the batch unit; inode/nlink |
| inv_fin01_q3_r2.csv | FIN01 | iso_offset | unit | sha256 | uid | UTF-8 with BOM; iso_offset times |
